# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

> ## `image-blaster`
> Creates 3D environments, SFX, and meshes from a single image using Claude skills, World Labs, and FAL. 
> 
> Can take you from an image to a fully meshed 3D environment in < 5 minutes, great for jumpstarting 3D work. Go full blast.
> 
> 
> ## Quickstart
> 
> 1. Open a Terminal, enter `git clone https://github.com/neilsonnn/image-blaster`
> 2. Enter the directory with `cd image-blaster`
> 3. Run `claude` (install with `curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash`)
> 4. Say hello to Claude, and give them your API key for [World Labs](https://platform.worldlabs.ai/) and [FAL](https://fal.ai/).
> 5. Put an image into `input/` directory and ask Claude to `blast it and confirm each step with me`.
> 
> ### Description
> 
> By default `image-blaster` will use your input image to create:
> 
> 1. 3D models (`.glb`, `.obj`) of all *dynamic* objects
> 2. Gaussian splat (`.spz`) of the *static* environment,
> 3. Ambient looping sound and object specific physics SFX (`.mp3`)
> 
> ### Extensions
> 
> You can embed `image-blaster` under the assets of *any game engine, DCC software, …[+3283 chars]

> Use this prompt/spec to create literal image and scene descriptions for downstream world, object, and sound generation.
> ## Goal
> Analyze one source image at a time and write a sibling JSON file next to it in `source/`. After every source image has a sibling JSON analysis, merge those flat records into the root `image.json`.
> Per-image JSON and root `image.json` use the same flat schema. Do not write an `images[]` array anywhere.
> ## Literal Description Rules
> - Describe the image like a technical scene survey.
> - Prefer concrete visible evidence over interpretation.
> - Do not write narrative phrases such as "feels like", "hints at", "suggests", "under surveillance", "relics", "mysterious", "lonely", or similar editorial framing.
> - Keep descriptions useful for generation prompts: subject, arrangement, material, color, shape, lighting, camera/view, and environmental effects.

Both steps for Screen 08 are done. The clean environment-only picture came out very close to your approved screen. The Marble 3D world did not: it doesn't match your approved layout.

**1. Clean environment-only Screen 08 (the updated preview above)**
- **The splash crest is gone.** I took only the crest area from the new fal.ai edit. That edit had re-rendered the whole picture again (6.6 levels of drift), so I didn't keep the rest of it.
- **Everything else is byte-identical** to the version you reviewed, checked pixel by pixel. That covers the towel, door, platform, spouts, rings, wall slots, foam, bubbles and lighting.
- **Exact 9:16 framing,** lined up perfectly with your approved frame (no shift, no zoom).
- **Scenery:** 92.4 % within 20 colour levels of your approved screen.

**2. Marble 3D world for Screen 08**
- **Cost:** 1,580 credits, as quoted; 5,420 remain. All files are saved: four splat qualities, the collider mesh, the 360° panorama.
- **Layout:** Marble turned your chamber into a **long, deep tunnel**. The door, towel and platform sit tiny at the far end, the river becomes a long narrow stream, and the four spout fixtures aren't in view. Around and behind the camera it also invented extra washer doors, more fixtures, extra gold rings and a second towel on a stool.
- **Matching score:** **6.4 %** of pixels within 20 levels of your approved screen (the clean picture was 92 %), structural similarity 0.24. I also searched the whole panorama for a view that matches your layout; the best was weak (0.38), and it pointed sideways.
- **Moving the 3D camera forward:** that doesn't recover your layout either. The picture smears, and then the camera ends up inside the water.
- **What does match:** the colours and materials (purple slotted metal, gold, cyan-pink water, foam).

This is the same result as your earlier Screen 05 Marble test. Marble reinvents the depth of a scene, so it can't reach your 95–100 % goal for the gameplay view. What it's useful for: a colour and material reference, a distant backdrop, and a rough room mesh for scale.

**My recommendation for your other playable screens (04, 05, 06, 07)**
- **Do** make clean environment-only pictures with fal.ai, the same way as Screen 08: one edit each, plus a cleanup edit if needed. The clean pictures are the best result of this test. They give your exact scenery without Benny, the HUD or coins, which is exactly what's needed to paint your approved look onto the game's own 3D scenery. That painting method is what took Screen 05 to 86.9 %.
- **Don't** spend Marble credits on them. They'd most likely come out like Screen 08.

**Your choices**
1. **Approve fal.ai clean pictures for 04, 05, 06 and 07?** That's 4 edits, plus a cleanup edit where needed. I'll show each one full-size before moving on.
2. **Start using the Screen 08 clean picture in the game?** I'd paint it onto the game's real Screen 08 scenery behind a test switch, leaving the normal game unchanged, then show you approved / before / new full-size.

Your fal.ai dashboard shows the exact charge for the two Screen 08 edits; fal.ai doesn't report it to me.

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-17T13:43:35.969Z

Phase 1 for Screen 08 is done: your approved scenery now sits on real 3D geometry in the game, behind the test switch `?match08=1`. The normal game is unchanged, pixel for pixel. Above are your three full-size previews (approved, current game, new) and the moving-camera montage.

**How close it is to your approved Screen 08** (share of pixels within 20 colour levels):

| | Current game | **New** |
|---|---|---|
| Scenery (everything except the HUD strip and Benny) | 3.3 % | **95.4 %** |
| Upper chamber: door, porthole, towel, platform, rings, upper spouts | 2.1 % | **98.5 %** |
| Lower river, foam, walls | 4.4 % | **98.5 %** |
| Middle area: start of the river, lower spouts, coins | 3.0 % | **83.5 %** |
| Whole screen, including Benny and the HUD | 4.0 % | **80.6 %** |

**What I built**
- **Real 3D shapes fitted to your picture:** the near pipe, gold arch, chamber walls, door wall, floor, a platform with rim and legs, the towel, and the four half-dome spouts. The chamber keeps the game's real dimensions, which already matched your picture.
- **Corrected river:** I traced your painted S-river and rebuilt the game's river path to follow it, so Benny and the coins now ride inside your painted river.
- **Your own pixels:** the texture is your original approved artwork everywhere it shows scenery (79 % of the picture). fal.ai's fill is used only where Benny, the HUD and the coins were removed.
- **Colours:** the game's colour processing is cancelled out on these surfaces, so your colours appear as painted.

**Moving camera and Android**
- **Riding forward and steering:** the chamber keeps real 3D depth. The door grows, the spouts and platform shift correctly, and Benny stays in the river.
- **One thing breaks:** once the camera passes the near gold arch, its edges show flat purple patches and some stretched gold. Those are surfaces your picture never showed. The fix is extra artwork for those inner faces and a proper metal material there.
- **Android:** about half the draw calls of the current Screen 08 (81 vs 160) and a cheap shader. The texture needs compressing before release (about 22 MB uncompressed → 2–4 MB).
- **Not yet tested on a real phone.** That's the next check.

**Still to fix for higher similarity**
1. Coin positions: the game's coins aren't where your painted coins are. They cause most of the middle-area gap.
2. Benny's look: his hat is bigger and his fur and pose differ from your painted Benny.
3. The arch's inner faces when the camera moves (above).

**Checks:** the gameplay test passes 8/8 with and without the switch, and everything is saved on GitHub (commit `61a5e36`). Phase 1 spent no fal.ai or World Labs credits. Full details are in `qa/match/08/NOTES.md`.

**Next:** once you've looked at the new Screen 08 and you're happy with the method, I'll start Phase 2. That's one fal.ai clean environment-only picture each for 04, 05, 06 and 07, each shown to you full-size, then the same 3D method for each screen. Or tell me first which Screen 08 issue to fix.

She carries the laundry to the washing machine and puts the clothes and towel containing Benny inside.

She doesn't know Benny is there.

She closes the washing machine and walks away. Benny remains inside, and this leads into the magical washing-machine adventure.

After Benny finishes his adventure, he must return to the laundry and hide inside the towel before the girl discovers he was missing.

The girl comes back and grabs the towel, completely unaware of Benny's adventure.

The ending must connect naturally to the beginning.

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-17T13:47:40.661Z

Your video plan is clear and now written into the project. I've also continued the Screen 08 work: your painted coins are now in the game, and the updated picture is above.

**Opening and ending videos**
- **Opening:** the girl carries the laundry, with Benny secretly hidden in the purple towel, loads it into the washer, closes it and walks away. That leads into the adventure.
- **Ending:** Benny gets back into the towel before she notices, and she grabs it, unaware. It loops naturally back to the beginning.
- **Recorded** in `docs/FLOW_VIDEO.md`, with a pointer in `CLAUDE.md`, so no future session rebuilds them as 3D.
- **The rules there:**
  - you make both videos in Google Flow; I never generate video or spend video credits
  - your characters, colours, laundry room and style stay the same
  - screens 04–08 stay real, playable 3D on Android
  - the videos hand over smoothly into and out of the game
  - your approved screens don't change

The game already has the hand-off built in. It crossfades from your opening video into the live game and plays your ending video before the final frame. Your `intro.mp4` and `ending.mp4` aren't in the project yet; when they are, they slot straight in.

**Screen 08 progress: coins**
- **Real coins on your painted coins.** Your picture's nine coins are now real, collectible game coins, placed exactly where your painting shows them along the river. Before, they followed an old pattern.
- **Higher match:**

  | | Before coins fix | Now |
  |---|---|---|
  | Scenery within 20 colour levels | 95.4 % | **96.8 %** |
  | Middle area (where the coins are) | 83.5 % | **90.1 %** |
  | Whole screen, including Benny and the HUD | 80.6 % | **82.0 %** |

- **Checks:** the gameplay test still passes 8/8, and the normal game is unchanged pixel for pixel.
- **One trade-off:** a full run now collects 82 coins instead of 91, because your 9 painted coins replace 17 older ones on screens 08–09.
- **Saved on GitHub:** commit `a85ccf9`, local and GitHub match.

**Still to fix on Screen 08**
1. **Benny's look:** his hat is bigger and his fur and splash differ from your painted Benny. Your other branch (`ccr-b0757dec-dj6l1o`) has the Screen 05 Benny artwork work that could be reused here once you decide about combining the branches.
2. **Moving camera:** when the camera passes through the near gold arch, its inner sides show flat purple. I plan to fill those with extra matching artwork.
3. **Coin spin:** the coins spin, so some are seen at an angle rather than face-on.

Next I'll fix the arch's inner sides for smooth movement, unless you'd rather I start Phase 2 (the clean pictures for 04–07) now.

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-17T14:11:27.557Z

Screen 08 is finished as far as I can take it from this cloud session. Above are six separate previews: your original, the improved screen, the same at phone size (1080×1920), and three moving-camera views (6 units forward, steering left, steering right). The one thing I couldn't do is run it on a real phone; the APK is ready for you to install.

**1. The gold arch when moving:** fixed. Where the camera sees a surface your picture never showed, it now gets tiles cut from your artwork: purple slotted metal, the gold band, towel fabric. The flat purple patches are gone. One mild leftover: a slightly darker purple band at the lower-left edge when the camera is 3–6 units forward.

**2. Benny:** closer, not yet exact.
- **Improved:** a smaller beanie in your proportions, white-and-purple knit stripes, his round body raised out of the water, and your painted white splash ring around him. The splash is cut from your original picture and rides with him.
- **Still different:** his body reads a bit wider than painted and sits about 30 px to the right; his fur and face are the game model's.
- **Score:** the Benny-and-splash area went from 61 % to 64.5 % similar.
- **Other branch:** I didn't take anything from your other branch (`ccr-b0757dec-dj6l1o`). Its Benny artwork was made for Screen 05's view, and that branch is untouched.

**3. Coins:** your nine painted coins now face the camera at their painted size and stay collectible. If one would be out of reach, it moves along its own line of sight, so it stays on your painted spot.

**4. Coin total:** preserved. The game again has **91 coins (2,240 total)**, exactly like the normal game. The 8 coins your picture doesn't show sit at the chamber entrance, where Benny collects them on the way in.

**5–6. Moving camera:** riding forward and steering left and right keep real 3D depth: the door grows, the spouts and platform shift, and Benny stays in the river.

**7. Android:**
- **Built:** `builds/rainbow-debug-match08-2026-11-17.apk` (21 MB) has Screen 08's new look built in.
- **Not run on a phone:** this cloud session has no phone or emulator.
- **To test:** install it on your phone and play to Screen 08.

**8. Safety:**
- your 13 approved images are unchanged
- the normal game is unchanged, pixel for pixel
- gameplay passes 8/8 with and without the switch
- everything is backed up on GitHub (commit `aa8cf44`)

**How close Screen 08 is now** (within 20 colour levels of your approved screen):

| | Current game | Now |
|---|---|---|
| Scenery | 3.3 % | **97.4 %** |
| Upper chamber | 2.1 % | **98.9 %** |
| River and walls | 4.4 % | **99.2 %** |
| Middle area with coins | 3.0 % | **91.0 %** |
| Benny and splash | 2.9 % | **64.5 %** |
| Whole screen | 4.0 % | **83.0 %** |

**Next:** Phase 2 (Screens 04–07) uses the same method, starting with one fal.ai clean picture each, shown to you full-size. Tell me whether Screen 08 is close enough to move on, or whether Benny's body should be refined first. And if you install the APK, let me know how it runs on your phone.
